     MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
     管理層討論與分析

     Business review                                                    業務回顧
     The release of the “Plan for Supporting the High-Quality           2022年底《支持國有企業辦醫療機構高質量
     Development of State-owned Enterprisesin Operating Medical         發展工作方案 》的出臺，凸顯了國企辦醫在
     Institutions” by the end of 2022 highlights the important          我國醫療服務行業中的重要地位和積極作
     position and positive role of state-owned enterprises in China’s   用。政策在要求國企醫院加快融入區域公共
     healthcare service industry. While requiring state-owned           衛生網絡的同時，也鼓勵國有企業創新辦醫
     hospitals to accelerate their integration into the regional        模式，發揮集團化運營管理優勢。在國家醫
     public healthcare network, the policy also encourages state-       改政策引導下，醫院發展已經從原來的重視
     owned enterprises to innovate their mode of operation and          單項指標突進，進入到全面均衡的高質量發
     give full play to their advantages in group operation and          展時代。而從行業發展客觀規律來看，集團
     management. Under the guidance of the national healthcare          化已被證明比單體醫院有著更高的發展上
     reform policy, the development of hospitals has moved from         限，將是醫療行業未來改革的重要方向。
     the original emphasis on a single indicator to a comprehensive
     and balanced era of high-quality development. From the
     perspective of the objective rule of industry development,
     group hospitalization has been proved to have a higher
     development ceiling than monolithic hospitals, and will be an
     important direction for the future reform of the healthcare
     industry.

     In 2023, the Group, as a leading state-owned hospital group        2023年，本 集 團 作 為 規 模 領 先 的 國 企 醫 院
     in terms of scale, gave full play to its advantages of grouping,   集團，充分發揮集團化優勢，加快融入地區
     accelerated its integration into the regional healthcare           醫療規劃版圖，以企業化機制充分釋放組織
     planning landscape, fully unleashed its organizational vitality    活力，以創新的方式提升學科建設、患者服
     through the entrepreneurial mechanism, and enhanced its            務、運營管理等核心能力，實現了量的合理
     core competencies in terms of disciplines construction, patient    增長和質的有效提升。報告期內，本集團通
     services and operation and management in an innovative             過「並購－整合－注入」的方式，於2023年6
     manner, thereby achieving reasonable quantitative growth           月底完成了對遼健標的和江能標的收購，進
     and effective qualitative enhancement. During the Reporting        一步擴大了旗下醫院網絡的佈局，為今後的
     Period, the Group completed the acquisitions of LH Subjects        高質量發展奠定了堅實基礎。
     and JE Subject by the end of June 2023 through the “merger-

Dividend Policy                                                   股息政策
According to the dividend policy of the Group, subject to         本集團之股息政策，乃在本集團盈利且不影
compliance with applicable rules and regulations (including       響本集團正常經營的情況下，遵守適用的規
Cayman Islands laws) and the Articles of Association, the         則 與 規 定（包 括 開 曼 群 島 法 律）與 組 織 章 程
Company will pay dividend to the Shareholders when the            細則由本公司向股東宣派股息。本公司擬以
Group recorded net profit during the period and payment of        年度股息形式與股東分享溢利，派息後的利
dividend would not affect its normal operation. The Company       潤餘額將用於本集團之發展與經營。
intends to share its profits with Shareholders in the form of
annual dividend and the remaining net profits will be used for
the Group’s development and operations.

The Company’s ability to pay dividends will depend upon,          本 公 司 派 發 股 息 的 能 力 取 決 於（其 中 包 括）
among other things, the general financial condition of the        本 集 團 之 一 般 財 務 狀 況、現 時 與 未 來 之 經
Group, the Group’s current and future operations, liquidity       營、流動資金狀況、資本需求以及自本公司
position and capital requirement of the Group as well as          之附屬公司收取之股息。本公司派發股息亦
dividends received from the Company’s subsidiaries. The           同時受限於開曼群島法律與組織章程細則
payment of the dividend by the Company is also subject            之任何限制所規限。
to any restrictions under the Cayman Islands laws and the
Articles of Association.

The dividend policy will continue to be reviewed from time        有關股息政策仍會不時檢討，不保證會在任
to time and there can be no assurance that dividends will be      何指定期間派付任何特定金額的股息。
paid in any particular amount for any given period.

     In order to provide more flexibility in the administration and     為了就管理及執行股份獎勵計劃提供更高
     implementation of the Share Award Scheme, the Board                靈活性，董事會於2015年5月25日議決修訂
     resolved on May 25, 2015 to make amendments to the                 股份獎勵計劃之條款及計劃規則，自採納日
     terms of the Share Award Scheme and the scheme rules,              期 起 追 溯 生 效，以 示 明 董 事 會 可 不 時 絕 對
     having retrospective effect from the Adoption Date, to the         酌 情 決 定 向 獲 選 參 與 者 授 出 獎 勵 股 份 時，
     effect that the Board may, from time to time, in its absolute      相關獲選參與者是否應付每股獎勵股份價格

YAN HUA IOT AGREEMENT DISPUTE                                     燕化IOT協議爭議
In respect of the litigation case between the Company             關於本公司與燕化方（即指燕化鳳凰及燕化
and the Yanhua Parties (i.e. the Yan Hua Phoenix and Yan          醫 院）就 繼 續 履 行 燕 化IOT協 議 於2019年 開
Hua Hospital) regarding the performance of the Yanhua             始 產 生 的 訴 訟，北 京 市 高 級 人 民 法 院 已 於
IOT Agreement in 2019, the Beijing Higher People’s Court          2021年11月22日 做 出 終 審 判 決（民 事 判 決
handed down a final judgment (2020 Jing Minzhong No.110)          書[2020]京民終110號），駁回燕化方於2020
on November 22, 2021, which rejected the appeal filed by          年提起的上訴，並維持2019年的一審判決，
Yanhua in 2020 and upheld the original judgment in 2019 as        即：主要內容如下：(1)宣告燕化鳳凰及燕化
follows: the major details are as follows: (1) the unilateral     醫院單方面對《燕化IOT協議 》作出的終止為
termination of Yan Hua IOT Agreement by Yan Hua Phoenix           無效，及雙方應繼續履行《燕化IOT協議 》項
and Yan Hua Hospital is declared to be void and that the          下的義務；(2)由燕化鳳凰向華潤醫院管理諮
parties shall continue to perform its obligations under the       詢支付金額為人民幣14,400,000元的因違反
Yan Hua IOT Agreement; (2) the amount of RMB14,400,000,           《燕 化IOT協 議 》引 起 的 損 害 賠 償；(3)駁 回
being the damages for breach of the Yan Hua IOT Agreement,        華 潤 醫 院 管 理 諮 詢 尋 求 的 其 他 賠 償；及(4)
is to be paid by Yan Hua Phoenix to CR Hospital Management        駁回燕化鳳凰和燕化醫院的反訴。由於燕化
& Consulting; (3) other reliefs sought by CR Hospital             方 始 終 怠 於 履 行 法 院 終 審 判 決，本 公 司 於
Management & Consulting shall be dismissed; and (4) other         2022年1月向北京市第二中級人民法院提起
counterclaims of Yan Hua Phoenix and Yan Hua Hospital shall       了強制執行的申請，並通過法院強制執行程
be dismissed. As the Yanhua Parties have been negligent           式於2022年4月收到燕化鳳凰支付的人民幣
in complying with the abovementioned final judgment, the          1,440萬 元 的 違 約 金 及 相 應 金 額 的 滯 納 金。
Company then filed an application for enforcement with            但由於燕化方堅持拒不履行燕化IOT協 議，
the Beijing Second Intermediate People’s Court in January         本 公 司 於2022年9月 向 燕 化 方 再 次 提 起 訴
2022, and then accordingly received the liquidated damages        訟，要求賠償本公司因燕化方違約而遭受的
amounted to RMB14,400,000 and related overdue fine                損失（包括由2019年至起訴時的本公司的應

      2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (continued)                                        2.1 編製基準（續）
           Basis of consolidation (continued)                                       綜合基準（續）
           Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of                     按 照 一 般 推 定，多 數 投 票 權 即 形 成 控
           voting rights results in control. When the Company has,                  制 權。當 本 公 司 直 接 或 間 接 擁 有 投 資
           directly or indirectly, less than a majority of the voting               對象少於大多數的投票權或類似權
           or similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all                利，本 集 團 於 評 估 其 對 投 資 對 象 是 否
           relevant facts and circumstances in assessing whether                    擁有權力時會考慮所有相關事實及情
           it has power over an investee, including:                                況，包括：

           (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote                    (a)   與投資對象的其他投票權持有人
                 holders of the investee;                                                 訂立的合約安排；

           (b)   rights arising from other contractual arrangements;                (b)   其他合約安排所產生的權利；及
                 and

           (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting                     (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。
                 rights.

           The financial statements of the subsidiaries are                         附屬公司之財務報表乃按與本公司相
           prepared for the same reporting period as the Company,                   同 的 報 告 期 間，採 用 一 致 的 會 計 政 策
           using consistent accounting policies. The results                        編 製。附 屬 公 司 之 業 績 乃 自 本 集 團 獲
           of subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on                        得 控 制 權 當 日 起 作 綜 合 入 賬，並 繼 續
           which the Group obtains control, and continue to be                      綜 合 入 賬，直 至 有 關 控 制 權 終 止 當 日
           consolidated until the date that such control ceases.                    為止。

           P r o f i t o r l o s s a n d e a ch c o m p o n e n t o f o t h e r     損益及其他全面收入的各組成部分乃
           comprehensive income are attributed to the owners of                     歸屬於本集團本公司擁有人及非控
           the Company of the Group and to the non-controlling                      股 權 益，即 使 此 舉 引 致 非 控 股 權 益 錄
           interests, even if this results in the non-controlling                   得 虧 損 結 餘。集 團 內 部 各 公 司 之 間 的
           interests having a deficit balance. Intra-group balances                 結 餘 及 交 易，以 及 集 團 內 部 各 公 司 之
           and transactions, and any unrealised income and                          間的交易所產生的未變現收入及開支
           expenses (except for foreign currency transaction                        （外幣交易收益或虧損外）均會對銷。
           gains or losses) arising from intra-group transactions,                  集團內部各公司之間的交易所產生未
           are eliminated. Unrealised losses resulting from intra-                  變 現 虧 損，會 以 與 未 變 現 收 益 相 同 的

           evidence of impairment.

2.4 SUMMARY OF MATERIAL                                      2.4 重大會計政策概要（續）
    ACCOUNTING POLICIES (continued)
   (b) Business combinations and goodwill                        (b) 業務合併及商譽（續）
       (continued)

       Any contingent consideration is recognised at                  任何或有代價將以收購日期的公
       fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent                 允價值確認。被分類為資產或負
       consideration classified as an asset or liability              債的或有代價按公允價值計量，
       is measured at fair value with changes in fair                 而公允價值變動於損益確認。或
       value recognised in profit or loss. Contingent                 有代價如被分類為權益項目，則
       consideration that is classified as equity is                  不再對其重新計量，後續的結算
       not remeasured and subsequent settlement is                    會計入權益中。
       accounted for within equity.

       Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the              商譽初步按成本計量，即轉讓代
       excess of the aggregate of the consideration                   價、確認為非控股權益的金額及
       transferred, the amount recognised for non-                    本集團此前持有的被收購方股權
       controlling interests and any fair value of the                的任何公允價值的和超出所收購
       Group’s previously held equit y interests in                   可識別資產淨值及所承擔負債
       the acquiree over the identifiable net assets                  的部分。倘該代價及其他項目的
       acquired and liabilities assumed. If the sum of                和低於所收購資產淨值的公允價
       this consideration and other items is lower than               值，有關差額在重估後於損益確
       the fair value of the net assets acquired, the                 認為議價收購收益。
       difference is, after reassessment, recognised in
       profit or loss as a gain on bargain purchase.

                the liability takes place either in the principal             市場進行或於未有主要市場的情

                 The transferred asset and the associated liability            利及責任的基準計量。
                 are measured on a basis that reflects the rights
                 and obligations that the Group has retained.

                 Continuing involvement that takes the form of a               若以就已轉讓資產作出保證的形
                 guarantee over the transferred asset is measured              式持續涉及，則按該項資產之原
                 at the lower of the original carrying amount of the           賬面值與本集團或須償還的代價
                 asset and the maximum amount of consideration                 數額上限兩者中之較低者計量。
                 that the Group could be required to repay.

           (k) Impairment of financial assets                            (k) 金融資產減值
                 The Group recognises an allowance for expected                本集團就並非以公允價值計量且
                 credit losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not           其變動計入損益的所有債務工具
                 held at fair value through profit or loss. ECLs are           確認預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧
                 based on the difference between the contractual               損」）的撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基
                 cash flows due in accordance with the contract                於根據合約到期的合約現金流量
                 and all the cash flows that the Group expects to              與本集團預期收取的所有現金流
                 receive, discounted at an approximation of the                量之間的差額而釐定，並以原實
                 original effective interest rate. The expected cash           際利率的近似值貼現。預期現金
                 flows will include cash flows from the sale of                流量將包括出售所持抵押品或組
                 collateral held or other credit enhancements that             成合約條款的其他信貸提升措施
                 are integral to the contractual terms.                        的現金流量。

       General approach                                                一般方法
       ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                   預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。
       exposures for which there has not been a                        就自首次確認起未有顯著增加的
       significant increase in credit risk since initial               信貸風險而言，須為未來12個月
       recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses                內可能發生違約事件而導致的信
       that result from default events that are possible               貸 虧 損（ 12個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損）
       within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For                 計提預期信貸虧損。就自初始確
       those credit exposures for which there has been                 認起經已顯著增加的信貸風險而
       a significant increase in credit risk since initial             言，不 論 何 時 發 生 違 約，於 餘 下
       recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit            風險年期內的預期信貸虧損均須
       losses expected over the remaining life of the                  計 提 虧 損 撥 備（全 期 預 期 信 貸 虧
       exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default             損）。
       (a lifetime ECL).

       At each reporting date, the Group assesses                      於各報告日期，本集團評估金融
       whether the credit risk on a financial instrument               工具的信貸風險自首次確認以來
       has increased significantly since initial                       是 否 大 幅 增 加。進 行 評 估 時，本
       recognition. When making the assessment, the                    集團會考慮合理及有理據且毋須
       Group considers reasonable and supportable                      花費過度成本或精力即可獲得的
       information that is available without undue                     資料，包括以本集團過往的經驗
       cost or effort, including both quantitative and                 及 有 依 據 的 信 用 評 估（包 括 前 瞻
       qualitative information and analysis, based on                  性 信 息）為 基 礎 的 定 量 和 定 性 資
       the Group’s historical experience and informed                  料及分析。
       credit assessment, that includes forward-looking
       information.

       The Group considers a financial asset in default                倘合約付款已逾期90日，則本集
       when contractual payments are 90 days past due.                 團 認 為 金 融 資 產 違 約。然 而，在
       However, in certain cases, the Group may also                   若干情況下，倘內部或外部資料
       consider a financial asset to be in default when                顯示，在沒有計及本集團所持有
       internal or external information indicates that                 的任何信貸提升措施前，本集團
       the Group is unlikely to receive the outstanding                不大可能悉數收到未償還合約款
       contractual amounts in full before taking into                  項，則本集團亦可認為金融資產
       account any credit enhancements held by the                     違約。倘無法合理預期收回合約
       Group. A financial asset is written off when there              現金流量，則撇銷金融資產。
       is no reasonable expectation of recovering the
       contractual cash flows.

   The pre-tax discount rate applied to the cash flow              應用於現金流量預測的稅前貼現率為
   projections is 15% (2022: 15%), which reflected                 15%（ 2022年︰15% ），反映了各現金
   specific risks relating to each CGU Group. The growth           產 生 單 位 組 別 的 相 關 獨 有 風 險。用 於
   rate used to extrapolate the cash flows beyond the five-        推斷超過五年期之現金流量的增長率
   year period is 2.5% (2022: 3%). This growth rate does           為2.5%（ 2022年︰3% ）。該增長率不
   not exceed the long-term average growth rates for the           超過相關市場上的長期平均增長率。
   relevant markets.

   Since 2022, Sinophi CGU Group has been experiencing a           自2022年起，Sinophi現金產生單位逐

           The sensitivity analysis above has been determined                           上文呈列的敏感度分析是根據一種方
           based on a method that extrapolates the impact on                            法 而 得 出，該 方 法 可 推 斷 由 於 在 報 告
           defined benefit obligations as a result of reasonable                        期末主要假設發生合理變化而對界定
           changes in key assumptions occurring at the end of                           福 利 責 任 產 生 的 影 響。敏 感 度 分 析 基
           the reporting period. The sensitivity analysis is based                      於 重 要 假 設 的 變 化，而 所 有 其 他 假 設
           on a change in a significant assumption, keeping all                         則 維 持 不 變。上 文 呈 列 的 敏 感 度 分 析
           other assumptions constant. The sensitivity analysis                         未 必 反 映 界 定 福 利 責 任 的 實 際 變 化，
           may not be representative of an actual change in the                         因為假設不大可能獨立發生變化。
           defined benefit obligation as it is unlikely that changes
           in assumptions would occur in isolation of one another.

           Liquidity risk                                                         流動資金風險
           Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will encounter               流動資金風險指本集團因資金短缺而
           difficulty in meeting financial obligations due to                     難 以 履 行 財 務 責 任 的 風 險。本 集 團 面
           shortage of funds. The Group’s exposure to liquidity risk              臨的流動資金風險主要來自金融資產
           arises primarily from mismatches of the maturities of                  和 負 債 的 到 期 日 錯 配。本 集 團 通 過 考
           financial assets and liabilities. The Group monitors its               慮金融負債和金融資產的到期日來監
           risk to a shortage of funds by considering the maturities              控資金短缺的風險。附註2.1說明管理
           of both its financial liabilities and financial assets.                層 管 理 本 集 團 流 動 資 金 需 要，使 其 能
           Note 2.1 explains management’s plan in managing the                    應付到期責任的計劃。
           liquidity needs of the Group to enable it to continue to
           meet its obligations as they fall due.

           The Group’s objective is to maintain a balance between                 本集團的目標是通過使用銀行借款在
           continuity of funding and flexibility through the use                  資金的連續性和靈活性之間保持平
           of bank borrowings. The Group aims to maintain                         衡。本 集 團 旨 在 維 持 足 夠 的 現 金 及 現
           sufficient cash and cash equivalents to meet its liquidity             金等價物，以滿足其流動資金需求。
           requirements.

   assets. To maintain or adjust the capital structure,                           可 能 調 整 向 股 東 派 付 的 股 息、退 回 股

   shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue

   of interest-bearing liabilities (excluding payables to                        集 團 舉 辦 之 醫 院 款 項）除 以 總 資 產 的
